# META
~~~ini
description=Dimension compatibility for quantity expressions under SysML 9.8.9.1
specification=OMG SysML 2.0
specification_url=https://www.omg.org/spec/SysML/2.0/Language/PDF
type=file
libraries=standard
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package QuantityExpressions {
    calc def calculateLoiDeltaV {
        in v_inf :> ISQ::speed;
        in mu_Moon :> ISQ::force;
        in r_periapsis :> ISQ::length;
        return deltaV :> ISQ::speed =
            (v_inf^2 + 2*mu_Moon/r_periapsis)^(1/2) - (mu_Moon/r_periapsis)^(1/2);
    }

    calc def consistent {
        in speed :> ISQ::speed;
        return result = speed^2 + speed*speed;
    }

    calc def compare {
        in force :> ISQ::force;
        in mass :> ISQ::mass;
        return result = -force <= mass;
    }

    calc def unknown {
        in speed :> ISQ::speed;
        in input;
        return result = speed + input;
    }

    attribute expression = 2 [SI::kg] - 3 [SI::m];
}
~~~
